\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|{\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\bigg(\prod_{i=1}^{j-1}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{i},s_{i})\Omega_{i}\bigg)\bigg({\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s_{j})-{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s_{j})\bigg)\Omega_{j}\bigg(\prod_{i=j+1}^{n}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}^{\delta_{2}}(t_{i},s_{i})\Omega_{i}\bigg)|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle}\right|,

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\left|{\int_{s_{j}}^{t_{j}}\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j-1}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s)(B_{\alpha,\sigma}^{\delta_{1}}(s)-B_{\alpha,\sigma}^{\delta_{2}}(s))R^{\sigma}_{\alpha}(s){\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{2}}(s,s_{j})\Omega_{j}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}\bigg)|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle ds}\right|

where, for notational convenience, we have defined

\mathcal{V}^{\delta}_{j,j^{\prime}}=\prod_{i=j}^{j^{\prime}}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}(t_{i},s_{i})\Omega_{i}.

Now, using the Wick’s theorem, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\bigg(\prod_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}(t_{i},s_{i})\bigg)|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle-\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\bigg(\prod_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}(t_{i},s_{i})\bigg)|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j,j^{\prime}\in[1:n]\\
j\neq j^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}_{j}\\
\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\left|{\Gamma_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}}\right|+\sum_{j\in[1:n]}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}_{j}\\
\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\bigg(\left|{\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{+,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}}\right|+\left|{\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{-,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}}\right|\bigg),

(231)

where for
j\neq j^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle\Gamma_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}=\begin{cases}\int_{s=s_{j}}^{t_{j}}\int_{s^{\prime}=s_{j^{\prime}}}^{t_{j^{\prime}}}\Delta_{+;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}-s)f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)ds^{\prime}ds&\text{ if }j^{\prime}<j,\\
\int_{s=s_{j}}^{t_{j}}\int_{s^{\prime}=s_{j^{\prime}}}^{t_{j^{\prime}}}\Delta^{\sigma^{\prime};\sigma}_{-,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}-s)f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)ds^{\prime}ds&\text{ if }j^{\prime}>j\end{cases}

with

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{+;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}(s)=K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{1}}_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s)-K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{2}}_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s),\ \Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{-;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}(s)=K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{2}}_{\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(-s)-K^{\delta_{2},\delta_{2}}_{\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(-s),

\displaystyle\text{For }j^{\prime}<j:

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)=\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}^{\delta_{1}}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\Omega_{j}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{0}}\rrangle,

\displaystyle\text{For }j<j^{\prime}:

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)=\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\Omega_{j}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,j^{\prime}-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j^{\prime}+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{0}}\rrangle.

and,

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{+,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}=\int_{s=s_{j}}^{t_{j}}\int_{s^{\prime}=s}^{t_{j}}\Delta_{+,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}-s)f_{+,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)ds^{\prime}ds,

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{-,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}=\int_{s=s_{j}}^{t_{j}}\int_{s^{\prime}=s_{j}}^{s}\Delta_{-,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}-s)f_{-,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)ds^{\prime}ds,

where

\displaystyle f_{+,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)=\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j-1}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s^{\prime})R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}){\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s){\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\Omega_{j}\mathcal{V}_{j+1,n}^{\delta_{2}}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle,

\displaystyle f_{-,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)=\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j-1}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s){\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{2}}(s,s^{\prime})R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}){\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s^{\prime},s_{j})\Omega_{j}\mathcal{V}_{j+1,n}^{\delta_{2}}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle.

Next, we separately upper bound
|{\Gamma_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}}|
and
|{\Gamma_{\pm,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}}|
. For this, we will use lemmas
3
and
30
.

Smallness of
\Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}
. First, we use lemma
3
to establish that
\Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}
is “small” — the test function space that we will employ will be
\text{PC}_{t^{*}}^{1}(\mathbb{R})
where
t^{*}=\{t_{j}-t_{j^{\prime}},s_{j}-s_{j^{\prime}},t_{j}-s_{j^{\prime}},s_{j}-t_{j^{\prime}}:j,j^{\prime}\in[1:n]\}
. We introduce
\tau=\max\left|{t}\right|
for
t\in t^{*}
. Now, consider
\Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{+;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}
and
f\in\text{PC}_{t^{*}}^{1}(\mathbb{R})
:

\displaystyle\left|{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{+;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}(s)f(s)}\right|=\left|{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}(K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{1}}_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s)-K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{2}}_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s))f(s)ds}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\left|{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\big(K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{1}}_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s)-K_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s)\big)f(s)ds}\right|+\left|{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\big(K^{\delta_{1},\delta_{2}}_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s)-K_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma}(s)\big)f(s)ds}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu2\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}2\bigg(\sum_{t\in t^{*}}\text{TV}(K_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma;c};[t-2\delta_{1},t+2\delta_{1}])+\text{TV}(K_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma;c};[t-\delta_{1}-\delta_{2},t+\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}])\bigg)\left\|{f}\right\|_{\infty}+

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\bigg(2\delta_{1}\text{TV}(K_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma};[-\tau-2\delta_{1},\tau+2\delta_{1}]+(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})\text{TV}(K_{\alpha^{\prime},\alpha,\sigma};[-\tau-\delta_{1}-\delta_{2},\tau+\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}]\bigg)\left\|{f^{\prime}}\right\|_{\infty},

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu3\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}4\bigg(\sum_{t\in t^{*}}u_{c}(t-2\delta,t+2\delta)\left\|{f}\right\|_{\infty}+\delta\ u(-\tau-2\delta,\tau+2\delta)\bigg)\left\|{f^{\prime}}\right\|_{\infty},

where in (1) we have used the triangle inequality, in (2) we have used lemma
3
and in (3) we have re-expressed our results in terms of the upper bounding on the total variation Assumption
1
and
\delta=\max(\delta_{1},\delta_{2})
. A similar analysis would yield an identical upper bound on the kernel
\Delta^{\nu,\sigma;\nu^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}_{-;\alpha}
. Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\Delta^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm;\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}\text{ is }\bigg(4\sum_{t\in t^{*}}u_{c}(t-2\delta,t+2\delta),4\delta\ u(-\tau-2\delta,\tau+2\delta)\bigg)\text{-close on the test-function space }\text{PC}_{t^{*}}^{1}(\mathbb{R}).

(232)

|{\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}}|,|{\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}}|
as
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\to 0
. We will now use the smallness of
\Delta_{\pm}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}
together with lemma
30
to show that
|{\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime};\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}}|,|{\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}}|\to 0
as
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\to 0
. In order to apply lemma
30
, we need to establish that
f^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}},f^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}
are bounded functions and have bounded derivatives with respect to either of its coordinates. We will show this in detail for
f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}
(with
j^{\prime}<j
) — a similar calculation will hold for
f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}
(with
j^{\prime}>j
) and
f_{\pm,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}
.

We begin by noting that, for any
s,s^{\prime}
,

|{f^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}|\leq\|{R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})}\|\|{R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)}\|\leq 1,

i.e.
f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)
is bounded above by a constant uniform in
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}
. The same holds for the derivative of
f^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}
with respect to either of its arguments. For instance, consider the derivative with respect to the first argument:

\displaystyle\left|{\partial_{1}f^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq|\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})\big(R_{\alpha’,\sigma^{\prime}})^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{0}}\rrangle|+

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})|\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime})},R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})]{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s){R}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{s}}\rrangle|+

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})|\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})[R_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime}),R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})]{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s){R}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle|,

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}|\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})\big(R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}})^{\prime}(s^{\prime})\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{0}}\rrangle|+

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})|\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime})},R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})]{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s){R}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{s}}\rrangle|+

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})|\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime});B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})\big)[R_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime}),R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})]{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s){R}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle|+

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})|\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime})[R_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime}),R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})]\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime});{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s){R}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle\big)|,

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\|{(R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}))^{\prime}}\|+4\left|{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}\right|+4\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime});B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})\big)}\|+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad 4\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}(\alpha^{\prime\prime})\left\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime});{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j},s){R}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle\big)}\right\|_{1},

(233)

where we obtained (1) by applying the Wick’s theorem. Now, note from Eq.
27
that

\displaystyle\left\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime});{\mathcal{U}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j^{\prime}}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime},s_{j^{\prime}})\Omega_{j^{\prime}}\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{1}}_{j^{\prime}+1,j-1}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{1}}(t_{j},s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}^{\delta_{2}}(s,s_{j})\mathcal{V}^{\delta_{2}}_{j+1,n}|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle\big)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime},\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime}}\bigg(\left|{\int_{s_{j^{\prime}}}^{s^{\prime}}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|+\sum_{l=j-1}^{j^{\prime}+1}\left|{\int_{s_{l}}^{t_{l}}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left|{\int_{s}^{t_{j}}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|+\left|{\int_{s_{j}}^{s}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{2}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\left|{\int_{s_{l}}^{t_{l}}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{2}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|\bigg),

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}4\big(n-j^{\prime}+6\big)L\left|{\mathcal{A}}\right|u(-\tau-2\delta,\tau+2\delta),

where, in (1), we have used Eq.
36
to conclude that for any
\delta,\delta^{\prime}\in\{\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\}
and interval
[a,b]\subseteq[-\tau,\tau]
,

\left|{\int_{a}^{b}\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha,\sigma}^{\delta}(s)B_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}^{\delta^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle ds^{\prime}}\right|\leq\text{TV}(K^{\delta,\delta^{\prime}}_{\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}};[a-2\delta,b+2\delta])\leq u(-\tau-2\delta,\tau+2\delta).

Similarly, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}(\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\mathcal{V}_{1,j^{\prime}-1}^{\delta_{1}}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}(t_{j^{\prime}},s^{\prime});B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})\big)}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\bigg(\left|{\int_{s^{\prime}}^{t_{j^{\prime}}}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|+\sum_{l=1}^{j^{\prime}-1}\left|{\int_{s_{l}}^{t_{l}}\left|{\llangle{I_{E}}|B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime\prime\prime})B_{\alpha^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime}}^{\delta_{1}}(s^{\prime})|{\rho_{E}}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime\prime\prime}}\right|\bigg),

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 4j^{\prime}L\left|{\mathcal{A}}\right|u(-\tau-2\delta,\tau+2\delta).

Combining these bounds with Eq.
233
, we obtain that

\left|{\partial_{1}f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}\right|\leq 4|{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}|+16(n-6)L|{\mathcal{A}_{S_{\alpha^{\prime}}}}||{\mathcal{A}}|\ u(-\tau-1,\tau+1)+\sup_{s^{\prime}\in\bigcup_{i=1}^{n}[s_{i},t_{i}]}\|{(R_{\alpha^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}))^{\prime}}\|,

which is bounded above uniformly in
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}
and
s,s^{\prime}
if
\delta<1/2
. A similar calculation can be performed to also check that
|{\partial_{2}f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}|
is also bounded above uniformly in
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}
and
s,s^{\prime}
. Since all
|{f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}|,|{\partial_{1}f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}|
and
|{\partial_{2}f_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},s)}|
are uniformly bounded, it then follows from the smallness of
\Delta_{+,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}
and lemma
30
b that
\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}\to 0
as
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\to 0
for
j>j^{\prime}
. A similar argument can be repeated for
\Gamma_{j,j^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}
(when
j<j^{\prime}
) as well as for
\Gamma^{\sigma;\sigma^{\prime}}_{\pm,j,\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}
(where we would use lemma
30
a instead of lemma
30
b). Together with Eq.
231
, this implies that

\left|{\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\bigg(\prod_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{1}}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}(t_{i},s_{i})\bigg)|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle-\llangle{I_{E},O_{S}}|\bigg(\prod_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{U}}^{\delta_{2}}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}(t_{i},s_{i})\bigg)|{\rho_{E},\sigma_{S}}\rrangle}\right|\to 0\text{ as }\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\to 0,

which, by the completeness of complex numbers, implies the lemma statement.
∎

Appendix B
Addressing the integrability and lower Lipschitz boundedness of the commutator bound

We now provide rigorous proofs justifying the discussion in
consideration
1 and
consideration
2 of section
IX.2
. In particular, lemma
32
proves that
\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta},\mathcal{B}}
is integrable in the sense discussed in
consideration
1. Finally, we also provide a proof of lemma
9
from section
IX.2
which addresses consideration 2.

To prove lemma
32
, we first prove a useful technical lemma.

Lemma 31
.

Suppose
A_{x}
is a family of possibly uncountable sets parameterized by
x\in[a,b]
and
g:A\times[a,b]\to[0,1]
, where
A=\cup_{x\in[a,b]}A_{x}
, is a function which satisfy the following properties:

1.

If
x\leq x^{\prime}
,
A_{x}\subseteq A_{x^{\prime}}
.

2.

\forall\phi\in A
,
g(\phi,\cdot)
is an
\ell-
Lipschitz continuous function, with
\ell
is uniform in
\phi
i.e.
\forall\ \phi\in A,\forall\ x,x^{\prime}\in[a,b]
,
\left|{g(\phi,x)-g(\phi,x^{\prime})}\right|\leq\ell\left|{x-x^{\prime}}\right|

Then the function
G:[a,b]\to\mathbb{R}
defined by

G(x)=\sup_{\phi\in A_{x}}g(\phi,x),

is Lesbesgue integrable.

Proof.

We note that
G
, by construction, is a bounded function over a compact interval
[a,b]
. Consequently, as long as
G
is measurable, it is also integrable. We will use two facts from measure theory to prove the measurability of
G
—

Fact 1:

Suppose
h:[a,b]^{2}\to\mathbb{R}
is a measurable function, then the function
H:[a,b]\to\mathbb{R}
defined by
H(x)=h(x,x)
is also a measurable function.

Fact 2:

Suppose
\{f_{n}:\mathcal{S}\to\mathbb{R}\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
, where
\mathcal{S}
together with an appropriate
\sigma-
algebra is a measurable space, is a sequence of measurable functions which converges pointwise to
f:\mathcal{S}\to\mathbb{R}
i.e.
f(x)=\lim_{n\to\infty}f_{n}(x)
, then
f
is also a measurable function.

To prove this lemma, we begin by defining
\tilde{G}:[a,b]^{2}\to[0,1]
via

\tilde{G}(x,y)=\sup_{\phi\in A_{x}}g(\phi,y),

then, clearly,
G(x)=\tilde{G}(x,x)
. Furthermore,
\tilde{G}(x,y)
satisfies two important properties —
first
, it is a non-decreasing function of
x
for any fixed
y\in[a,b]
. To see this, simply note that for
x\leq x^{\prime}
, since
A_{x}\subseteq A_{x^{\prime}}

\tilde{G}(x,y)=\sup_{\phi\in A_{x}}g(\phi,y)\leq\sup_{\phi\in A_{x^{\prime}}}g(\phi,y)=\tilde{G}(x^{\prime},y).

Second
,
\tilde{G}(x,y)
is continuous in
y
for any fixed
x\in[a,b]
. To see this, note that for any
y,y^{\prime}

\tilde{G}(x,y)=\sup_{\phi\in A_{x}}g(\phi,y)\leq\sup_{\phi\in A_{x}}\bigg[g(\phi,y^{\prime})+\ell\left|{y-y^{\prime}}\right|\bigg]\leq\tilde{G}(x,y^{\prime})+\ell\left|{y-y^{\prime}}\right|.

Since this holds for any
y,y^{\prime}
, it also implies that
\tilde{G}(x,y^{\prime})\leq\tilde{G}(x,y)+\ell\left|{y-y^{\prime}}\right|
and thus
\left|{\tilde{G}(x,y^{\prime})-\tilde{G}(x,y)}\right|\leq\ell\left|{y-y^{\prime}}\right|
, which implies continuity of
\tilde{G}(x,\cdot)
.

Next, note that since
\tilde{G}(x,y)
is continuous in
y
, we can approximate by a sequence of functions
\tilde{G}_{n}(x,y)
where

\tilde{G}_{n}(x,y)=\sum_{j=-\infty}^{\infty}\Theta_{[(j-1)/n,j/n)}(y)\tilde{G}\bigg(x,\frac{j}{n}\bigg),

i.e. for every
x
, we perform a step-like approximation of
\tilde{G}(x,\cdot)
. It follows from the continuity of
\tilde{G}(x,\cdot)
that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\tilde{G}_{n}(x,y)=G(x,y).

Now, we establish that
\tilde{G}_{n}(x,y)
is measurable. Recall that this is equivalent to showing that
\tilde{G}_{n}^{-1}((-\infty,a])
is measurable —
\tilde{G}_{n}^{-1}((-\infty,a])
can be explicitly calculated to be

\tilde{G}_{n}^{-1}((-\infty,a])=\bigcup_{j=-\infty}^{\infty}\mathcal{X}_{n,j}\times\bigg[\frac{j-1}{n},\frac{j}{n}\bigg)\text{ where }\mathcal{X}_{n,j}=\bigg\{x:G\bigg(x,\frac{j}{n}\bigg)\leq a\bigg\}.

We note that
\mathcal{X}_{n,j}\subseteq[a,b]
is measurable — to see this, note that since
G(\cdot,j/n)
is a non-decreasing function, if
x\in\mathcal{X}_{n,j}
and
x^{\prime}\leq x
, then
x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}_{n,j}
and hence
\mathcal{X}_{n,j}
can only be
(-\infty,b]
or
(-\infty,b)
for some b or
(-\infty,\infty)
. From the measurability of
\mathcal{X}_{n,j}
, it immediately follows that
\tilde{G}_{n}^{-1}((-\infty,a])
is measurable implying that
\tilde{G}_{n}
is a measurable function.

Finally, we then use Fact 2 to conclude that
\tilde{G}(x,y)
, which is a point-wise limit of
\tilde{G}_{n}(x,y)
, is also measurable and use Fact 1 to finally conclude that
G(x)=\tilde{G}(x,x)
is measurable.
∎

Lemma 32
.

Given
s_{0}<s_{1}
and
\bm{\zeta}=\{\zeta_{\alpha}\}
with
\zeta_{\alpha}:\mathbb{R}\to[0,\infty)
a locally bounded continuous function,

(a)

Given
s_{0}<s_{1}
, the function
\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}_{[s_{0},\cdot]}^{R},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\cdot):[s_{0},s_{1}]\to\mathbb{R}
is Lesbesgue integrable.

(b)

Given a smooth and integrable function
k:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{C}
, the function
h:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{C}
defined via

h(s)=\int_{s_{0}}^{s_{1}}k(s-s^{\prime})\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}_{[s_{0},s^{\prime}]}^{R},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s^{\prime})ds^{\prime},

is integrable.

Proof.

(a) For notational convenience, we begin by defining
\Gamma(\cdot)=\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}_{[s_{0},\cdot]}^{R},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\cdot)
and rewriting it as

\Gamma(s)=\sup_{\phi\in A_{s}}g(\phi,s)\text{ where }A_{s}=\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]}}(\rho_{E})\text{ and }g(\phi,s)=\sup_{\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|,\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\leq 1}\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|.

We now show that the sets
A_{s}
and the function
g
satisfy the properties required for an application of lemma
31
. To show property 1 of lemma
31
, we first note that since for
s<s^{\prime}
and
\forall\tau\in\mathbb{R}
,

\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha,[s_{0},s]}(\tau)=\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha}(\tau-s,\tau-s_{0})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha}(\tau-s^{\prime},\tau-s_{0})=\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha,[s_{0},s^{\prime}]}(\tau),\quad\forall\alpha,

where in (1) we have used the definition of
\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha}
in Eq.
67
. Therefore, from the definition of
S_{\bm{\zeta}}(\rho_{E})
[Definition
6
], it follows that
A_{s}\subseteq A_{s^{\prime}}
. To show property 2 of lemma
31
, suppose
\phi\in\cup_{s\in[s_{0},s_{1}]}A_{s}
—we note that for
s,s^{\prime}\in[s_{0},s_{1}]
with
s^{\prime}<s
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle-\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad=\left|{\int_{s^{\prime}}^{s}\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau)\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}}(\tau)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle d\tau}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{\alpha}\bigg(\Theta_{\mathcal{B}}(\alpha)\left|{\int_{s^{\prime}}^{s}\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau)\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha}(\tau)}\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle d\tau}\right|+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\Theta_{\mathcal{B}}(\alpha)\left|{\int_{s^{\prime}}^{s}\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau)B_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle d\tau}\right|\bigg),

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}2\left|{\mathcal{B}}\right|\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\|_{\diamond}\left|{s-s^{\prime}}\right|+\sum_{\alpha,\sigma}\Theta_{\mathcal{B}}(\alpha)\bigg[\int_{s^{\prime}}^{s}\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau);B_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau)\big)}\|\|{R_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau)}\|_{\diamond}\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\|_{1}d\tau+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\int_{s^{\prime}}^{s}\|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau)}\|\|{R_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau)}\|_{\diamond}\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau);\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle\big)}\|_{1}d\tau\bigg],

(234)

where we have used the Wick’s theorem in (1). Next, we note that
\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau)\in\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[\tau,s_{1}]}}(\rho_{E})
—from lemma
7
, it then follows that
\forall\tau\in[s^{\prime},s]
:

\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\tau);B_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau)\big)}\|\leq 2\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\mu^{\textnormal{L}}_{\alpha,\sigma,[\tau,s_{1}]}(\tau)=2\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\mu^{\textnormal{L}}_{\alpha,\sigma}(0,s_{1}-\tau)\leq 2\mu^{\textnormal{L}}_{\alpha,\sigma}(s_{0}-s_{1},s_{1}-s_{0}).

Similarly, suppose that
\phi\in A_{\tilde{s}}
for some
\tilde{s}
—then, note that
\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle\in\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},\tilde{s}]}}(\rho_{E})
. It then follows from lemma
6
c that
\forall\tau\in[s^{\prime},s]
:

\displaystyle\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha,\sigma}(\tau);\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle\big)}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}(\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},\tilde{s}]}(\tau)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}(\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha}(\tau-\tilde{s},\tau-s_{0})),

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\left(\sup_{\tau\in[s_{0},s_{1}]}\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)+\beta_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha}(s_{0}-s_{1},s_{1}-s_{0})\right).

From these estimates and Eq. (
234
), it follows that

\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle-\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|\leq\ell\left|{s-s^{\prime}}\right|,

where

\ell=\left|{\mathcal{B}}\right|\bigg(2+4L\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(s_{0}-s_{1},s_{1}-s_{0})}\right\|_{\infty}+2L\beta_{0}\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(s_{0}-s_{1},s_{1}-s_{0})}\right\|_{\infty}+2L\sup_{\alpha,\tau\in[s_{0},s_{1}]}\zeta_{\alpha}(\tau)\bigg).

Consider now

\displaystyle g(\phi,s)

\displaystyle=\sup_{\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\leq 1,\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\leq 1}\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|,

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\sup_{\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\leq 1,\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\leq 1}\bigg(\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|+\ell\left|{s-s^{\prime}}\right|\bigg),

\displaystyle\leq g(\phi,s^{\prime})+\ell\left|{s-s^{\prime}}\right|,

where in (1) we have used Eq.
234
. A similar analysis would also yield that
g(\phi,s^{\prime})\leq g(\phi,s)+\ell\left|{s-s^{\prime}}\right|
and consequently we obtain that
\left|{g(\phi,s^{\prime})-g(\phi,s)}\right|\leq\ell\left|{s-s^{\prime}}\right|
. This implies that
g(\phi,\cdot)
is a continuous function. Finally, since the sets
A_{s}
and the function
g
satisfy the properties of the lemma
31
, it then follows that
\Gamma
is also an integrable function.

(b) The proof of part a justifies that
\gamma_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},\cdot]},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\cdot):[s_{0},s_{1}]\to[0,1]
is an integrable function and thus allows us to sensibly define and use integrals of the form

\int_{s_{0}^{\prime}}^{s_{1}^{\prime}}g(s)\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s)ds,

where
s_{0}\leq s_{0}^{\prime}\leq s_{1}^{\prime}\leq s_{1}
and
g:[s_{0},s_{1}]\to\mathbb{R}
is an integrable and function. Consider now the function

h(s)=\int_{s_{0}^{\prime}}^{s_{1}^{\prime}}k(s-s^{\prime})\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s^{\prime}]},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s^{\prime})ds^{\prime},

where
k
is a smooth and integrable function. We note that
h(s)
is well defined since
k(s-(\cdot))\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},\cdot]},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},\cdot)
is integrable in the interval
[s_{0}^{\prime},s_{1}^{\prime}]
. Furthermore, as a function of
s,s^{\prime}
,
k(s-s^{\prime})\gamma^{X,Y}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s^{\prime}]},\mathcal{B}}(s_{1},s^{\prime})
is absolutely integrable and hence by Fubini’s theorem,
h
is itself an integrable function.
∎

Next, we address
consideration
2 from section
IX.2
by providing a proof of lemma
9
.

Proof of lemma
9
.

The proof of this lemma follows by repeated application of Wick’s theorem. We begin by noting that for
\phi\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s-\varepsilon]}}(\rho_{E})

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad=\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s)\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s,s-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad=\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle-i\int_{s-\varepsilon}^{s}\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime})\mathcal{H}(s^{\prime})\mathcal{U}(s^{\prime},s-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{{\phi}}\rrangle ds^{\prime}}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|+\Theta_{\mathcal{B}}(\alpha)\int_{s-\varepsilon}^{s}\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime})B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})|{\tilde{\phi}(s^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime}+2\varepsilon\left|{\mathcal{B}}\right|,

where, for
s^{\prime}\in[s-\varepsilon,s]
,
|{\tilde{\phi}(s^{\prime})}\rrangle=\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s^{\prime},s-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle
. First, note that since
\phi\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s-\varepsilon]}}(\rho_{E})\subseteq\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]}}(\rho_{E})
, it follows that
\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|\leq\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\gamma_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]}}^{X,Y}(t,s)
and therefore

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\gamma_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]}}^{X,Y}(t,s)+\Theta_{\mathcal{B}}(\alpha)\int_{s-\varepsilon}^{s}\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime})B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})|{\tilde{\phi}(s^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|ds^{\prime}+2\varepsilon\left|{\mathcal{B}}\right|.

(235)

Next, note that since
\phi\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s-\varepsilon]}}(\rho_{E})
,

\tilde{\phi}(s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+\beta_{0}\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s-\varepsilon]}+\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s-\varepsilon,s^{\prime}]}}(\rho_{E})\subseteq\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+(\beta_{0}+1)\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s^{\prime}]}}(\rho_{E})\subseteq\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+(\beta_{0}+1)\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]}}(\rho_{E}),

and consequently,

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime})B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})|{\tilde{\phi}(s^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\left|{\overleftarrow{\text{W}}(\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime}),B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime}))R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}({s^{\prime}})}\rrangle}\right|+\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime})R_{\alpha,\sigma}\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overrightarrow{\text{W}}\big(B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime}),\tilde{\phi}(s^{\prime})\big)}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left\|{\overleftarrow{\text{W}}(\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,s^{\prime}),B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime}))}\right\|\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}+\left\|{\overrightarrow{\text{W}}(B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime}),|{\tilde{\phi}({s^{\prime}})}\rrangle)}\right\|_{1}\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu2\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\big(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(0,t-s^{\prime})}\right\|_{\infty}+\zeta_{\alpha}(s^{\prime})+(\beta_{0}+1)\mu_{\alpha,[s_{0},s]}^{R}(s^{\prime})\big)\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu3\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\big(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(0,t-s_{0})}\right\|_{\infty}+\sup_{\alpha,s^{\prime}\in[s_{0},t]}\zeta_{\alpha}(s^{\prime})+(\beta_{0}+1)\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(s_{0}-t,t-s_{0})}\right\|_{\infty}\big)\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond},

(236)

where in (1) we have used the Wick’s theorem, in (2) we have used lemma
7
together with
\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}(t,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[s^{\prime},t]}}(\rho_{E})
to upper bound
\left\|{\overleftarrow{\text{W}}(\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},s^{\prime}),B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime}))}\right\|
and lemma
6
c to upper bound
\left\|{\overrightarrow{\text{W}}(B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s^{\prime}),|{\tilde{\phi}({s^{\prime}})}\rrangle)}\right\|_{1}
. In (3), we have used the fact that the function
\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha,[s_{0},s]}(s^{\prime})
, for any
s_{0}<s,s^{\prime}<t
, is upper bounded by
\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{\alpha}(s_{0}-t,t-s_{0})
.
From Eqs.
235
and
236
, we then obtain that

\gamma^{X,Y}_{\zeta+\alpha_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s-\varepsilon]},_{\mathcal{B}}}(t,s-\varepsilon)\leq\gamma^{X,Y}_{\zeta+\alpha_{0}\mu^{\textnormal{R}}_{[s_{0},s]},_{\mathcal{B}}}(t,s)+C\varepsilon,

with

C=\left|{\mathcal{B}}\right|\left(2+4\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}\right)

which proves the lemma statement.
∎

Appendix C
Proof of lemma
10

Proof of lemma
10
.

Define
|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle=\mathcal{U}_{Y}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle
and note that
|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+2\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]}}(\rho_{E})
. We will assume that
\left\|{\mathcal{L}_{Y}}\right\|_{\diamond},\left\|{O_{X}}\right\|=1
without loss of generality since the quantity that we want to bound is linear in
\mathcal{L}_{Y},O_{X}
. Using the triangle inequality, we then obtain that the error of interest has two contributions:

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\phi}\rrangle-\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\bigg(I-i\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s)ds\bigg)\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|\leq\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})+\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})

(237a)

where

\displaystyle\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})=\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\big(\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})-\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})\big)\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|,

(237b)

\displaystyle\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})=\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\bigg(\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}-\bigg(I-i\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s)ds\bigg)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\bigg)|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|.

(237c)

We will analyze both of these errors separately and provide
O(\varepsilon^{2})
upper bounds on them.

Analyzing
\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})
. This error can be analyzed by performing a first-order Dyson series expansion of
\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})
and
\mathcal{U}_{Y}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\mathcal{U}_{Y}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})=\mathcal{L}_{Y}-i\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}[\mathcal{H}(s^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]ds^{\prime}+\mathcal{R}^{(2)}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon),

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{Y}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)\mathcal{L}_{Y}\mathcal{U}_{Y}(t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime})=\mathcal{L}_{Y}-i\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}[\mathcal{H}_{Y}(s^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]ds^{\prime}+\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(2)}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon),

(238)

where
\mathcal{R}^{(2)}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon),\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{Y}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)
are the respective remainders that are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)=(-i)^{2}\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}\int_{s_{1}^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},s_{2}^{\prime})[\mathcal{H}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(s_{2}^{\prime},t^{\prime})ds_{2}^{\prime}ds_{1}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(2)}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)=(-i)^{2}\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}\int_{s_{1}^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(t^{\prime},s_{2}^{\prime})[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{2}^{\prime},t^{\prime})ds_{2}^{\prime}ds_{1}^{\prime}.

We remind the reader that

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s)=\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s)+\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s)\text{ where }\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s)=\Theta_{{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}}(\alpha)\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha}(s)},\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s)=\Theta_{{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}}(\alpha)(-1)^{\sigma}B_{\alpha,\sigma}(s)R_{\alpha,\sigma}(s),

(239)

Consequently,
[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha)(s),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]=[\mathcal{H}(s),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]
. Therefore,
\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})
[Eq.
237
b] has contributions only from the second order remainder in the Dyson expansion in Eq.
238
i.e.

\displaystyle\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})\leq\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|+\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{R}^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|.

(240)

Consider first bounding
\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|
— note that

\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|=\left|{\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}\int_{s_{1}^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}}\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha)(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle ds_{2}^{\prime}ds_{1}^{\prime}}\right|,

where, for
s_{2}^{\prime}\in[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]
,
\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|=\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(t^{\prime},s_{2}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t]}}(\rho_{E})
and
|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle=\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{2}^{\prime},t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle\in\mathcal{S}_{\bm{\zeta}+2\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]}}(\rho_{E})
. Now, note that

\displaystyle\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{Y}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle=\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi(s_{2}^{\prime})}}\rrangle+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle+\llangle{\hat{\theta(s_{2}^{\prime})}}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle.

We can bound these terms one by one. For calculating these bounds, it will be convenient to note that, from lemmas
6
c and
7
, it follows

\displaystyle\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}}\big)}\|\leq 2^{2}\prod_{j=1,2}\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t]}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}\leq 4\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\|_{\infty}^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}},|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\big)}\|_{1}\leq\prod_{j=1,2}\left(2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}\right)\leq\left(2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\|_{\infty}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\big)}\|\leq 2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t]}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}\leq 2\left(\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\|_{\infty}\right),

\displaystyle\|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime}),|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\big)}\|_{1}\leq\|{2\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}\leq\left(2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\|_{\infty}\right).

(241)

Now, note that

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\left|{\llangle{\theta(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})},[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})},\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|\leq 2^{4}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}.

(242)

Next,

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}^{S}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}^{SE}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})},[B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma}(s_{1}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\left|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma}(s_{1}^{\prime}))[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{2}}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle}\right|+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})},[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}(B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma},|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle)}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu2\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}2^{4}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}L\left(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}\right),

(243)

where, in (1), we have used the Wick’s theorem and in (2) we have used the bounds in Eq.
241
. A similar analysis yields the same bound for
\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle
—

\displaystyle\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\leq 2^{4}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}L\left(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}\right).

(244)

Finally, consider
\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle
. We note that

\displaystyle\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle

\displaystyle=\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})(-1)^{\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2}}\bigg(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle-

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\bigg),

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{=}}\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})(-1)^{\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2}}\bigg(\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}}\big)[R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}},|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\big)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})\big)[R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\big)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\big)[R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\big)\bigg)+

\displaystyle\quad\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\bigg((-1)^{\sigma_{1}}K_{\alpha_{2},\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{2}^{\prime}-s_{1}^{\prime})\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{C}_{R_{\alpha_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})}[R_{\alpha,\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad(-1)^{\sigma_{2}}K_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{1}^{\prime}-s_{2}^{\prime})\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{C}_{R_{\alpha_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})},\mathcal{L}_{Y}]R_{\alpha,\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle\bigg),

(245)

where (1) is obtained by using the Wick’s theorem and writing out all possible wick contractions of the bath operators
\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}}
, with the contractions being with either
|{\hat{\phi}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\rrangle,\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|
or between themselves (i.e. the last line of Eq.
245
).
Next, using the bounds in Eq.
241
together with the fact that
\left|{K_{\alpha_{2},\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{2}^{\prime}-s_{1}^{\prime})}\right|,\left|{K_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{1}^{\prime}-s_{2}^{\prime})}\right|\leq\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}
(lemma
2
a), we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|[\mathcal{H}^{SE}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),[\mathcal{H}^{SE}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{Y}]]}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}L^{2}\bigg(2^{6}\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}+2^{4}(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})^{2}+

\displaystyle\quad\quad 2^{6}\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})+2^{4}\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}\bigg).

(246)

Combining the bounds in Eqs.
242
,
243
,
244
and
246
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\varepsilon^{2}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}\big(2^{4}L^{2}\left((2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})^{2}+\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{5}L\left(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+2^{4}\big).

(247a)

A similar analysis can be carried out to bound
\left|{\mathcal{R}^{(2)}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)}\right|
and obtain

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{R}^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t^{\prime},t^{\prime}-\varepsilon)|{\phi}\rrangle}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\varepsilon^{2}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}^{\prime}}\right|\big(2^{4}L^{2}\left((2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})^{2}+\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{5}L\left(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+2^{4}\big).

(247b)

where
\mathcal{A}^{\prime}_{Y}=\{\alpha^{\prime}:\mathcal{S}_{\alpha}\cap S_{\alpha^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\text{ for some }\alpha\in\mathcal{A}_{Y}\}
. From Eq.
240
, we then obtain that

\displaystyle\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\big(\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|(\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|+\left|{\mathcal{A}^{\prime}_{Y}}\right|)\big(2^{4}L^{2}\left((2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})^{2}+\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+

\displaystyle\qquad 2^{5}L\left(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+2^{4}\big).

Analyzing
\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})
. Next, we bound
\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})
— starting from Eq.
237b
can be expressed as

\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})=\left|{\int_{t^{\prime}-\varepsilon}^{t^{\prime}}\int_{s_{1}^{\prime}}^{t^{\prime}}\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle ds_{2}^{\prime}ds_{1}^{\prime}}\right|,

where as defined above,
\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|=\llangle{O_{X},I_{E}}|\mathcal{U}_{\mathcal{B}}(t,t^{\prime})\mathcal{U}_{Y}(t^{\prime},s_{2}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[s_{2}^{\prime},t]}}(\rho_{E})\subseteq\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t]}}(\rho_{E})
. Using the explicit expression for
\mathcal{H}_{Y}(s)
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle=\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle+\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle+\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle.

In addition to the bounds in Eq.
241
, it will be useful to also note that

\displaystyle\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime});|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle\big)}\|_{1}\leq 2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}\leq\big(2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\|_{\infty}\big),

\displaystyle\|{\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}};|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle\big)}\|_{1}\leq\prod_{j=1,2}\left(2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}_{[t^{\prime}-\varepsilon,t^{\prime}]}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}(s_{j}^{\prime})}\|_{\infty}\right)\leq\big(2\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\|_{\infty}\big)^{2}.

(248)

Now, first note that we have the bound

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|\leq\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})}\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})}\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|\leq 4\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}.

(249)

Next, consider

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|\leq\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{1}}(s_{1})}\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\bigg(\left|{\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}({\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})})R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})}\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{C}_{h_{\alpha_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})}\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}(B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle)}\right|\bigg),

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu2\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}2^{2}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}L\big(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}\big).

(250)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{S}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|\leq 2^{2}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}L\big(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}\big).

(251)

Finally, consider

\displaystyle\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=(-1)^{\sigma_{1}}(-1)^{\sigma_{2}}\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=(-1)^{\sigma_{1}}(-1)^{\sigma_{2}}\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})\bigg(\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}}\big)R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\{B_{\alpha_{j},\sigma_{j}}(s_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in\{1,2\}},|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle\big)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})\big)R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime}),|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle\big)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\overleftarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|,B_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\big)R_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})R_{\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overrightarrow{\textnormal{W}}_{\rho_{E}}\big(B_{\alpha_{2},\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime}),|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle\big)\bigg)+

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad(-1)^{\sigma_{1}}(-1)^{\sigma_{2}}\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{1})\Theta_{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}(\alpha_{2})K_{\alpha_{2},\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{2}^{\prime}-s_{1}^{\prime})\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|R_{\alpha,\sigma_{2}}(s_{2}^{\prime})R_{\alpha,\sigma_{1}}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle.

(252)

Therefore, using the bounds in Eqs.
241
and
248
together with the fact that
\left|{K_{\alpha_{2},\alpha_{1},\sigma_{1}}(s_{2}^{\prime}-s_{1}^{\prime})}\right|\leq\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|{\llangle{\hat{\theta}(s_{2}^{\prime})}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{2}^{\prime})\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{SE}(s_{1}^{\prime})\mathcal{L}_{Y}|{\tilde{\phi}}\rrangle}\right|\leq

\displaystyle 2^{2}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}L^{2}\big(2^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}+(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})^{2}+

\displaystyle\qquad 2^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})+\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}\big)

Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\left|{\mathcal{A}_{Y}}\right|^{2}\big(2^{2}+2^{3}L\left(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}\right)+

\displaystyle\qquad 2^{2}L^{2}\left(\left\|{K}\right\|_{\infty}+(2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{L}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+2\left\|{\bm{\mu}^{\textnormal{R}}(-t,t)}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\zeta}_{[0,t]}}\right\|_{\infty})^{2}\right)\big).

Combining the upper bounds for
\Delta^{(1)}(t,t^{\prime})
and
\Delta^{(2)}(t,t^{\prime})
, we obtain the lemma statement.
∎

Appendix D
Proof of Lemma
15

Proof of Lemma
15
.

(a) We note that, from Eq. (
100
) that

d(\textnormal{x}_{\alpha},\textnormal{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\leq l\implies d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\leq p_{+}l+q_{+}\quad\text{and}\quad d(\textnormal{x}_{\alpha},\textnormal{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\geq l\implies d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\geq\frac{1}{p_{-}}(l-q_{-}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\textnormal{x}_{\alpha},\textnormal{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\leq l}1\leq\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\leq p_{+}l+q_{+}}1=\sum_{n=0}^{\lfloor p_{+}l+q_{+}\rfloor}\left|{\{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})=n\}}\right|.

(253)

Next, note that

\left|{\{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})=n\}}\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\mathcal{Z}\left|{\{x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}:d(x,\mathcal{S}_{\alpha})=n\}}\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu2\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\mathcal{Z}\left|{\mathcal{S}_{\alpha}}\right|\left|{\{x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}:d(x,0)=n\}}\right|,

where in (1) we have used the fact that any point
x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
can be in at-most
\mathcal{Z}
sets
\mathcal{S}_{\alpha}
and (2) follows from the fact that, for any set
\mathcal{S}
,
d(x,\mathcal{S})=n\implies\exists y\in\mathcal{S}:d(x,y)=n
and thus
\left|{\{x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}:d(x,\mathcal{S})=n\}}\right|\leq\left|{\{x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d},y\in\mathcal{S}:d(x,y)=n\}}\right|\leq\left|{\mathcal{S}}\right|\left|{\{x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}:d(x,y)=n\}}\right|
. Next, each coordinate span of
\mathcal{S}_{\alpha}
is at most
a_{0}
, so
\left|{\mathcal{S}_{\alpha}}\right|\leq(a_{0}+1)^{d}
. Also,
\left|{\{x\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}:d(x,0)=n\}}\right|\leq 2^{d}d^{d-1}(1+n)^{d-1}
for every integer
n\geq 0
. Define
C_{d,a_{0}}=(a_{0}+1)^{d}2^{d}d^{d-1}
to collect these geometric factors. We obtain

\displaystyle\left|{\{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})=n\}}\right|\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}(1+n)^{d-1}.

(254)

Returning to Eq. (
253
), we then obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\textnormal{x}_{\alpha},\textnormal{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\leq l}1\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\sum_{n=0}^{\lfloor p_{+}l+q_{+}\rfloor}(1+n)^{d-1}\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}(1+p_{+}l+q_{+})^{d}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptscriptstyle(\mkern-1.5mu1\mkern-1.5mu)}}{{\leq}}\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}(1+p_{+}+q_{+})^{d}l^{d},

(255)

where (1) follows from the assumption that
l\geq 1
. Setting
D_{0}=\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}(1+p_{+}+q_{+})^{d}
, we obtain the first inequality in part (a) of the lemma statement.

Following a similar analysis, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}:l\leq d(\textnormal{x}_{\alpha},\textnormal{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}})<l+1}1

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}:\begin{subarray}{c}d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\leq p_{+}(l+1)+q_{+}\\
d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})\geq(l-q_{-})/p_{-}\end{subarray}}1,

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}:n\in[(l-q_{-})/p_{-},p_{+}(l+1)+q_{+}]}\left|{\{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})=n\}}\right|,

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}:n\in[(l-q_{-})/p_{-},p_{+}(l+1)+q_{+}]}(1+n)^{d-1},

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\bigg(\left|{p_{+}-\frac{1}{p_{-}}}\right|l+1+p_{+}+q_{+}+\frac{q_{-}}{p_{-}}\bigg)\big(1+p_{+}(l+1)+q_{+}\big)^{d-1},

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\left|{p_{+}-\frac{1}{p_{-}}}\right|(1+2p_{+}+q_{+})^{d-1}l^{d}+\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\bigg(1+p_{+}+q_{+}+\frac{q_{-}}{p_{-}}\bigg)(1+2p_{+}+q_{+})^{d-1}l^{d-1}.

Here we bound the number of integers in the interval by its length plus one, and use
l\geq 1
in the last step. Setting
D_{\partial 0}=\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}(1+p_{+}+q_{+}+q_{-}/p_{-})(1+2p_{+}+q_{+})^{d-1}
and
D_{\partial 1}=\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\left|{p_{+}-1/p_{-}}\right|(1+2p_{+}+q_{+})^{d-1}
proves the second inequality in part (a) of the lemma.

(b) We begin by noting that for any
c_{0}\in(0,1)
,

\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}}\frac{1}{(1+d(\text{x}_{\alpha},\text{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}}))^{\eta}}\leq\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}}\frac{1}{(1+c_{0}d(\text{x}_{\alpha},\text{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}}))^{\eta}}.

Picking
c_{0}<\min(1,p_{+}/q_{+})
and using Eq. (
100
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}}\frac{1}{(1+d(\text{x}_{\alpha},\text{x}_{\alpha^{\prime}}))^{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}}\frac{1}{((1-c_{0}q_{+}/p_{+})+(c_{0}/p_{+})d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}}))^{\eta}},

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\min((1-c_{0}q_{+}/p_{+}),c_{0}/p_{+})^{\eta}}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}}\frac{1}{(1+d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}}))^{\eta}}.

(256)

We now proceed similar to the analysis in part (a) to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}}\frac{1}{(1+d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}}))^{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(1+n)^{\eta}}\left|{\{\alpha^{\prime}:d(\mathcal{S}_{\alpha},\mathcal{S}_{\alpha^{\prime}})=n\}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(1+n)^{d-1}}{(1+n)^{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}\bigg(1+\frac{1}{\eta-d}\bigg).

(257)

Setting
c_{0}=\min(1,p_{+}/q_{+})/2
and

B_{\eta}=\frac{2^{\eta}\mathcal{Z}C_{d,a_{0}}}{\min((1-c_{0}q_{+}/p_{+}),c_{0}/p_{+})^{\eta}}\bigg(1+\frac{1}{\eta-d}\bigg),

we obtain part (b) of the lemma.

∎
